# All inactive puzzle questions

Supabase snapshot: 4 September 2026. All 800 inactive records, including destinations beyond the previous ten-place list. Exact saved content; no data changed.

All are multiple-choice questions from four legacy template families: Destination profile, Explorer check, Location detail, and Mystery clue (200 each). The generator contains a retirement rule setting questions with these prefixes inactive. This export does not establish the historical timestamp or actor for each change.
